## Transcripts

### greet-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 你好，你能帮我做什么？
  [partner message] 我是 Ducter，你的 AI 伙伴。比如你可以让我修好某个仓库里挂掉的测试。
```

### greet-en

```
--- step 1: user says: hi! what can you do?
  [partner message] I'm Ducter, your personal AI partner. For example, I can have a coding agent fix a failing test in one of your repos while you keep chatting with me.
```

### greet-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 안녕하세요. 오늘 어떤 일을 도와줄 수 있나요?
  [partner message] 안녕하세요! 저는 Ducter예요. 이 컴퓨터에서 예를 들면 깨져 있는 저장소 테스트를 고쳐 달라고 하시면 바로 맡아서 처리할 수 있어요.
```

### ok-keeps-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 고마워요. 내일 아침에 다시 얘기해요.
  [partner message] 안녕히 주무세요. 내일 아침에 다시 얘기해요.
--- step 2: user says: OK
  [partner message] 네, 내일 아침에 봐요.
```

### direct-ja

```
--- step 1: user says: 一言で：TCP と UDP の一番の違いは何ですか？
  [partner message] TCP と UDP の一番の違いは、**TCP は届いたことと順番を保証する（信頼性重視）のに対し、UDP は保証せず速さ・軽さを優先する**点です。
```

### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Usa **rebase** para ordenar tu rama local y privada antes de compartirla (historial lineal, limpiar commits). Usa **merge** cuando la rama ya es pública o la comparten otras personas, porque reescribir su historial las rompería.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？
  [partner message] TCP 是可靠的、面向连接的传输：数据有确认、丢了会重传、保证按顺序到达；UDP 则不保证这些，只管尽快发出去，速度快但可能丢包或乱序。
```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase when the branch is yours alone and not yet shared, to tidy your commits and bring it up to date before it lands. Merge when others have pulled the branch or it's already on the main line, because rebasing rewrites history and breaks their copies.
```

### mem-source-none-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?
  [partner message] A write-ahead log is an append-only file where a database records each change before applying it to the main data, so a crash can be recovered by replaying the log. This makes committed changes durable and atomic while letting the database write the actual data files lazily and in batches.
```

### past-compaction-pr-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 接着上周那个 compaction 的 PR，把 review 里说的锁粒度改细一点
  [partner ack line] 好，我接着把 compaction 那个 PR 里 review 提到的锁粒度改细。
  [sent to task compaction_pr] 用户原话：「接着上周那个 compaction 的 PR，把 review 里说的锁粒度改细一点」。

要做的：按 review 里提到的锁粒度问题，把 compaction 相关的加锁范围缩小（只锁真正需要互斥的部分），在原来的分支上继续提交。

完成标准：改动后相关测试通过，并把跑过的测试命令和结果报告回来；说明每处锁的范围改了什么。

不要做的：不合并，不把草稿 PR 标为 ready，不改 PR 的目标分支，不做 review 之外的重构。

地点：沿用这个任务原来的仓库和分支。
```

### past-rust-haswell-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 我上个月在 haswell 上那个 Rust 构建的，再接着把增量构建也弄快
  [partner ack line] 好，我先找上个月在 haswell 上那个 Rust 构建的任务，找到就接着把增量构建弄快。
  [sent to task rust_haswell] 用户原话："我上个月在 haswell 上那个 Rust 构建的，再接着把增量构建也弄快"

接着上次的工作：冷构建已从 14 分钟降到 6 分钟（sccache + mold），增量构建还没动。

要做的：把 haswell 上 Rust 的增量构建也弄快，比如检查 incremental 设置、sccache 对增量的命中、链接阶段（mold）、不必要的全量重编。

完成标准：给出改动前后的增量构建耗时（同一个改动、同一台机器上测），以及改动在哪个分支、做了什么。

不在范围内：不合并到主分支，不推送，不开 PR，除非用户之后明确说可以。在自己的分支上提交即可。
  [retitled task rust_haswell] haswell 上 Rust 构建提速：冷构建与增量构建
  [also used tools] task_find
```

  const list=document.getElementById("list");

  for(const x of xs){
    const row=document.createElement("div");row.className="k-row";
    const main=document.createElement("div");
    const what=document.createElement("div");what.textContent=x.what;
    const day=document.createElement("div");day.className="k-row-sub";day.textContent=x.day;
    main.appendChild(what);main.appendChild(day);
    const val=document.createElement("div");val.className="k-row-value";val.textContent="¥"+x.yuan;
    row.appendChild(main);row.appendChild(val);list.appendChild(row);
  }
  ducter.summary(xs.length+" 笔,合计 ¥"+sum.toLocaleString("zh-CN"));
}
ducter.onChange(draw);draw();

      <button class="k-btn primary" id="save">记下</button>
    </div>
  </details>
</main>

<style>
  .k-row { cursor: pointer; }
</style>

<script>
  function todayStr() {
    const d = new Date();
    const m = String(d.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0');
    const day = String(d.getDate()).padStart(2, '0');
    return d.getFullYear() + '-' + m + '-' + day;
  }
  function nowHM() {
    const d = new Date();
    return String(d.getHours()).padStart(2, '0') + ':' + String(d.getMinutes()). …  document.createElement('div');
      row.className = 'k-row';
      row.innerHTML =
        '<div class="k-row-main">' + f.time + '</div>' +
        '<div class="k-row-value">' + f.ml + ' ml</div>';
      row.addEventListener('click', () => removeFeed(f));
      box.appendChild(row);
    });
  }

  async function removeFeed(f) {
    const ok = await ducter.ask('删掉 ' + f.time + ' 的 ' + f.ml + ' ml 这一条？');
    if (!ok) return;
    feeds = feeds.filter(x => x !== f);
    ducter.set('feeds', feeds);
  }

  document.getElementById('save').addEventListener('click', () => {
    const t = document.getElementById('t').value.trim() || nowHM();
    const ml = Number(document.getElementById('ml').value);
    if (!/^\d{1,2}:\d{2}$/.test(t) || !ml || ml <= 0) return;
    feeds.push({ date: todayStr(), time: t, ml: ml });
    ducter.set('feeds', feeds);
    document.getElementById('ml').value = '';
    document.getElementById('t').value = '';
    document.querySelector('.k-add').open = false;
  });

  document.getElementById('t').placeholder = nowHM();

  ducter.onChange(() => {
    feeds = ducter.get('feeds') || [];
    render();
  });

  feeds = ducter.get('feeds') || [];
  render();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"feeds":[{"date":"2026-11-28","time":"06:10","ml":120},{"date":"2026-11-28","time":"09:05","ml":90}]}

```
(earlier) user: which of my PRs are still waiting on someone?
(earlier) partner: Four: wey-gu/ducter#412 (compaction cut) waits on Weizhen's review since Monday; nowledge-co/mem#10287 (retry on 502) on CI, failing; nowledge-co/hawdb#1592 (flaky compaction test) on Alice since Tuesday; wey-gu/ducter#418 (apps bar) is a draft.
--- step 1: user says: make that a little page I can keep and tick off as they land
  [partner message] Done: a page with the four PRs, and tapping a row ticks it off as it lands. It's on your shelf under "PRs waiting on someone."
  [an app card under the message: made "PRs waiting on someone", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 1745 characters): <main class="k-app">
  <div class="k-sum">
    <div class="k-sum-label">Still waiting</div>
    <div class="k-sum-value" id="open">4</div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">0 of 4 landed</div>
  </div>
  <h2 class="k-h">My PRs</h2>
  <div class="k-card">
    <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
  </div>
  <p class="k-from">Tap a row to tick it off when it lands.</p>
</main>
<script>
var prs = ducter.get("prs") || [];
function done() { return ducter.get("landed") || {}; }
function render() {
  var d = done();
  var list = document.getElementById("list");
  list.innerHTML = "";
  var n = 0;
  prs.forEach(function (p) {
    var on = !!d[p.id];
    if (on) n++;
    var row = document.createElement("button");
    row.className = "k-row";
    row.setAttribute("aria-pressed", on ? "true" : "false");
    row.style.width = "100%";
    row.style.textAlign = "left";
    row.style.background = "none";
    row.style.border = "0";
    row.style.color = "var(--d-ink)";
    row.style.cursor = "pointer";
    row.innerHTML =
      '<span class="k-row-main" style="' + (on ? "text-decoration:line-through;color:var(--d-muted)" : "") + '">' +
      '<span class="k-row-sub">' + (on ? "☑" : "☐") + " " + p.ref + "</span><br>" +
      p.title + '</span>' +
      '<span class="k-row-sub">' + (on ? "landed" : p.waiting) + "</span>";
    row.onclick = function () {
      var cur = done();
      cur[p.id] = !cur[p.id];
      ducter.set("landed", cur);
    };
    list.appendChild(row);
  });
  document.getElementById("open").textContent = String(prs.length - n);
  document.getElementById("sub").textContent = n + " of " + prs.length + " landed";
  ducter.summary(n + " of " + prs.length + " landed");
}
ducter.onChange(render);
render();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"prs":[{"id":"ducter-412","title":"Compaction cut","ref":"wey-gu/ducter#412","waiting":"Weizhen's review, since Monday"},{"id":"mem-10287","title":"Retry on 502","ref":"nowledge-co/mem#10287","waiting":"CI, failing"},{"id":"hawdb-1592","title":"Flaky compaction test","ref":"nowledge-co/hawdb#1592","waiting":"Alice, since Tuesday"},{"id":"ducter-418","title":"Apps bar","ref":"wey-gu/ducter#418","waiting":"Draft"}]}

<section class="k-sum"><span class="k-sum-label">出差花销合计</span><span class="k-sum-value" id="total"></span><span class="k-sum-sub" id="count"></span></section>
<h2 class="k-h">花销明细</h2>
<div class="k-card"><div class="k-list" id="list"></div></div>
<details class="k-add"><summary>记一笔</summary>
<div class="k-field"><label>出差名称</label><input class="k-input" id="trip" value="北京出差"></div>
<div class="k-field"><label>日期</label><input class="k-input" id="day" placeholder="如 11/20"></div>
<div class="k-chips" id="cats"><button class="k-chip" data-cat="交通" aria-pressed="true">交通</button><button class="k-chip" data-cat="住宿" aria-pressed="false">住宿</button></div>
<div class="k-field"><label>金额</label><input class="k-num" id="amt" inputmode="decimal" placeholder="0"></div>
<button class="k-btn primary" id="save">保存</button>
</details>
</main>
<script>
const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
let cat="交通";
const esc=s=>String(s??"").replace(/[&<>"]/g,c=>({"&":"&amp;","<":"&lt;",">":"&gt;",'"':"&quot;"}[c]));
const fmt=n=>"¥"+Number(n||0).toLocaleString("zh-CN");
function draw(){
  const xs=ducter.get("items")||[];
  const total=xs.reduce((s,x)=>s+Number(x.amt||0),0);
  $("total").innerHTML=xs.length?esc(fmt(total)):'<span class="k-none">还没记</span>';
  $("count").textContent=xs.length?xs.length+" 笔":"这次出差还没有花销";
  $("list").innerHTML=xs.length?xs.map(x=>'<div class="k-row"><span class="k-row-main">'+esc(x.cat)+'</span><span class="k-row-sub">'+esc(x.trip)+'</span><span class="k-row-sub">'+esc(x.day)+'</span><span class="k-row-value">'+esc(fmt(x.amt))+'</span></div>').join(""):'<p class="k-empty">还没有花销，点下面的「记一笔」加第一笔。</p>';
  ducter.summary(xs.length?xs.length+" 笔,合计 "+fmt(total):"还没记");
}
$("save").onclick=()=>{
  const amt=Number($("amt").value);
  if(!amt)return;
  const xs=ducter.get("items")||[];
  xs.push({trip:$("trip").value.trim(),day:$("day").value.trim(),cat:cat,amt:amt});
  ducter.set("items",xs);
  $("amt").value="";
  draw();
};
document.querySelectorAll("#cats .k-chip").forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{
  cat=b.dataset.cat;
  document.querySelectorAll("#cats .k-chip").forEach(c=>c.setAttribute("aria-pressed",String(c===b)));
});
ducter.onChange(draw);
draw();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"items":[{"trip":"北京出差","day":"11/20","cat":"交通","amt":662},{"trip":"北京出差","day":"11/20–21","cat":"住宿","amt":1036}]}

(earlier) partner: TTL 测试修好了：它用真实计时去等 1 秒过期，机器一忙就抢跑，现在改成假时钟。
(earlier) user: 再给 ~/dev/foo 的 Cache 加一个 values() 方法
(earlier) partner ack: 我去给 foo 的 Cache 加 values()。  → task "foo: 给 Cache 加 values()" done; its report: Added Cache.values() returning live values; 2 new tests, 14/14 pass.

--- step 1: user replies (by swipe, quoting it) to the earlier message with "TTL 测试修好了": 这个再补一条测试，覆盖正好到期那一刻
  [partner ack line] 好，我让 TTL 那个任务再补一条正好到期时刻的测试。
  [sent to task ttl] 用户原话：「这个再补一条测试，覆盖正好到期那一刻」

请在现有的假时钟 TTL 测试基础上，再加一条测试，覆盖条目恰好在过期时刻（时间等于 TTL 边界）的行为：
- 在正好到期的那一刻，值应当被视为已过期（或按现有实现的约定，明确断言边界行为）。
- 到期前一毫秒仍然有效。
- 用假时钟推进，不要用真实计时。

完成标准：新测试通过，并且整套 TTL 测试连续多次运行都稳定通过；报告里附上测试输出。
范围外：不要合并、不要推送、不要开 PR。改动留在现有的分支上提交。
地点：~/dev/foo，分支 ducter/foo-ttl。
